Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in complex theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 659 and the invariant I(A,B) = 203 mod 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the complex decomposition. By minimality, the element a^659 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 203-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the complex space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 394 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 336. QED.
